Here the target comes after necessary; in the previous sentence it came before.\'] },\\r\\n150: ];\\r\\n151: export function TargetReader({ item }: { item: TargetExample }) {\\r\\n152:   const [choice, setChoice] = useState<number | null>(null);\\r\\n153:   const correct = choice === item.correct;\\r\\n154:   const id = useId();\\r\\n155:   const firstTarget = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);\\r\\n156:   return <div className=\\"cr-target co-lab\\"><p className=\\"co-kicker\\">{item.label}</p><blockquote>{item.before}{correct ? <mark>{item.target}</mark> : item.target}{item.after}</blockquote><h3 id={id}>Which complete idea does the indicator refer to?</h3><div className=\\"cr-answer-options\\" role=\\"group\\" aria-labelledby={id}>{item.options.map((option, index) => <button ref={index === 0 ? firstTarget : undefined} key={option} type=\\"button\\" aria-pressed={choice === index} onClick={() => setChoice(index)}>{option}</button>)}</div><div role=\\"status\\">{choice !== null && <div className={`cr-feedback ${correct ? \'cr-correct\' : \'cr-revise\'}`}><strong>{correct ? \'Correct target.\' : \'Not quite.\'}</strong><p>{item.feedback[choice]}</p></div>}</div>{choice !== null && <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cr-reset\\" onClick={() => { firstTarget.current?.focus(); setChoice(null); }}>Clear the target</button>}</div>;\\r\\n157: }\\r\\n158: \\r\\n159: type PracticeItem = { sentence: string; left: string; right: string; leftCode: string; rightCode: string; leftNegated?: boolean; rightNegated?: boolean; originalPosition: number; contraPosition: number; why: string; readback: string; contraReadback: string };\\r\\n160: export const referencePracticeItems: PracticeItem[] = [\\r\\n161:   { sentence: \'If a parcel has a red tracking tag, its sender has paid for tracking.\', left: \'Parcel has red tracking tag\', right: \'Sender has paid for tracking\', leftCode: \'RT\', rightCode: \'PT\', originalPosition: 1, contraPosition: 2, why: \'The if-clause is enough to establish payment. A sender who paid could have a parcel without this tag; payment alone does not settle the tag.\', readback: \'If a parcel has a red tracking tag, its sender has paid for tracking.\', contraReadback: \'If its sender has not paid for tracking, the parcel does not have a red tracking tag.\' },\\r\\n162:   { sentence: \'Every applicant who submits by Friday receives a confirmation.\', left: \'Applicant submits by Friday\', right: \'Applicant receives confirmation\', leftCode: \'SF\', rightCode: \'RC\', originalPosition: 2, contraPosition: 0, why: \'Keep who submits by Friday with applicant. The rule gives those applicants confirmations; it does not restrict all confirmations to them.\', readback: \'If an applicant submits by Friday, the applicant receives a confirmation.\', contraReadback: \'If an applicant does not receive a confirmation, the applicant did not submit by Friday.\' },\\r\\n163:   { sentence: \'A document is stored in the archive only if it has a catalogue number.\', left: \'Document stored in archive\', right: \'Document has catalogue number\', leftCode: \'A\', rightCode: \'C\', originalPosition: 0, contraPosition: 1, why: \'Only if identifies the catalogue number as required. Deletion requires the backup; having a backup does not establish deletion.\', readback: \'If a file is deleted, it has a backup copy.\', contraReadback: \'If a file has no backup copy, it is not deleted under this rule.\' },\\r\\n165:   { sentence: \'Only guests with prepaid tickets enter the balcony.\', left: \'Guest enters balcony\', right: \'Guest has prepaid ticket\', leftCode: \'E\', rightCode: \'P\', originalPosition: 2, contraPosition: 1, why: \'Only restricts balcony entrants to guests with prepaid tickets. A ticketed guest can stay outside, so the ticket alone is not enough.\', readback: \'If a guest enters the balcony, the guest has a prepaid ticket.\', contraReadback: \'If a guest does not have a prepaid ticket, the guest does not enter the balcony.\' },\\r\\n166:   { sentence: \'The only recipes printed on the weekly menu are vegan recipes.\', left: \'Recipe printed on weekly menu\', right: \'Recipe is vegan\', leftCode: \'M\', rightCode: \'V\', originalPosition: 0, contraPosition: 2, why: \'The only recipes printed on the weekly menu names the group whose recipes must be vegan. Other vegan recipes can be absent from the menu.\', readback: \'If a recipe is printed on the weekly menu, it is vegan.\', contraReadback: \'If a recipe is not vegan, it is not printed on the weekly menu.\' },\\r\\n167: \\r\\n168:   { sentence: \'If a parcel has not cleared customs, it stays in the holding room.\', left: \'Parcel has cleared customs\', right: \'Parcel stays in holding room\', leftCode: \'C\', rightCode: \'H\', leftNegated: true, originalPosition: 2, contraPosition: 1, why: \'Not having cleared customs is enough to establish the holding room. Negating not cleared removes the not.\', readback: \'If a parcel has not cleared customs, it stays in the holding room.\', contraReadback: \'If a parcel does not stay in the holding room, it has cleared customs.\' },\\r\\n169:   { sentence: \'If the display is active, its status light is not red.\', left: \'Display is active\', right: \'Status light is red\', leftCode: \'A\', rightCode: \'R\', rightNegated: true, originalPosition: 1, contraPosition: 2, why: \'An active display requires a light that is not red. A locked door rules out that not-reserved condition, establishing a reservation. Reversing and negating removes both existing not-signs; it does not make reservation enough for locking.\', readback: \'If a room is not reserved, its door is not locked.\', contraReadback: \'If a room’s door is locked, the room is reserved.\' },\\r\\n171: ];\\r\\n172: function practiceDiagrams(item: PracticeItem) {\\r\\n173:   const left = `${item.leftNegated ? \'~\' : \'\'}${item.leftCode}`;\\r\\n174:   const right = `${item.rightNegated ? \'~\' : \'\'}${item.rightCode}`;\\r\\n175:   const notLeft = `${item.leftNegated ? \'\' : \'~\'}${item.leftCode}`;\\r\\n176:   const notRight = `${item.rightNegated ? \'\' : \'~\'}${item.rightCode}`;\\r\\n177:   return { original: `${left} → ${right}`, contra: `${notRight} → ${notLeft}`, reverse: `${right} → ${left}`, inverse: `${notLeft} → ${notRight}` };\\r\\n178: }\\r\\n179: function practiceChoices(item: PracticeItem, contrapositive: boolean) {\\r\\n180:   const diagrams = practiceDiagrams(item);\\r\\n181:   const correct = contrapositive ? diagrams.contra : diagrams.original;\\r\\n182:   const wrong = [diagrams.reverse, diagrams.inverse];\\r\\n183:   const position = contrapositive ? item.contraPosition : item.originalPosition;\\r\\n184:   const options = [...wrong];\\r\\n185:   options.splice(position, 0, correct);\\r\\n186:   return options;\\r\\n187: }\\r\\n188: export function IntegratedItem({ item, number, onChecked }: { item: PracticeItem; number: number; onChecked?: (checked: boolean) => void }) {\\r\\n189:   const [original, setOriginal] = useState<number | null>(null);\\r\\n190:   const [contra, setContra] = useState<number | null>(null);\\r\\n191:   const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);\\r\\n192:   const correct = original === item.originalPosition && contra === item.contraPosition;\\r\\n193:   const labels = [{ code: item.leftCode, meaning: item.left }, { code: item.rightCode, meaning: item.right }];\\r\\n194:   if (number % 2 === 0) labels.reverse();\\r\\n195:   const change = (value: number, isContra: boolean) => { setChecked(false); onChecked?.(false); if (isContra) setContra(value); else setOriginal(value); };\\r\\n196:   const reset = () => { setOriginal(null); setContra(null); setChecked(false); onChecked?.(false); };\\r\\n197:   return <div className=\\"cr-integrated-item co-check\\" data-reference-practice={number}><header><span className=\\"cr-item-number\\">{String(number).padStart(2, \'0\')}</span><h3>{item.sentence}</h3></header><dl className=\\"cr-practice-legend\\">{labels.map(label => <div key={label.code}><dt>{label.code}</dt><dd>{label.meaning}</dd></div>)}</dl>\\r\\n198:     <fieldset><legend>Original diagram for item {number}</legend><div className=\\"cr-answer-options cr-symbol-options\\">{practiceChoices(item, false).map((option, index) => <button key={option} type=\\"button\\" aria-pressed={original === index} onClick={() => change(index, false)}>{option}</button>)}</div></fieldset>\\r\\n199:     <fieldset><legend>Contrapositive for item {number}</legend><div className=\\"cr-answer-options cr-symbol-options\\">{practiceChoices(item, true).map((option, index) => <button key={option} type=\\"button\\" aria-pressed={contra === index} onClick={() => change(index, true)}>{option}</button>)}</div></fieldset>\\r\\n200:     <div className=\\"cr-control-row\\"><button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cr-primary\\" disabled={original === null || contra === null} aria-label={`Check both diagrams for item ${number}`} onClick={() => { setChecked(true); onChecked?.(true); }}>Check both diagrams</button><button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cr-reset\\" onClick={reset}>Try item {number} again</button></div>\\r\\n201:     <div role=\\"status\\">{checked && <div className={`cr-feedback ${correct ? \'cr-correct\' : \'cr-revise\'}`}><strong>{correct ? \'Both diagrams are correct.\' : \'Review the relationship.\'}</strong><p>{original === item.originalPosition ? \'Your original preserves the direction.\' : \'Your original changes the direction or negates conditions the sentence did not negate.\'} {contra === item.contraPosition ? \'Your contrapositive reverses and negates both complete conditions.\' : \'Your contrapositive must start from the missing requirement and rule out the sufficient condition.\'}</p><p>{item.why}</p><p className=\\"cr-answer-key\\">Original: {practiceDiagrams(item).original}<br/>Contrapositive: {practiceDiagrams(item).contra}</p><p>Read the original: {item.readback}</p><p>Read its contrapositive: {item.contraReadback}</p></div>}</div>\\r\\n202:   </div>;\\r\\n203: }\\r\\n204: \\r\\n205: export function conditionalReferenceContent() {\\r\\n206:   return <div className=\\"cr-sequence\\">\\r\\n207:     <ReferenceStep number={1} label=\\"Start with meaning\\" title=\\"What does a conditional tell you?\\">\\r\\n208:       <p>A <strong>condition</strong> is an idea that can be true or false. You win a raffle. You get a bicycle. We can ask whether either condition holds in a particular case.</p>\\r\\n209:       <p>A <strong>conditional statement</strong> tells us that if one condition is true, another has to be true too. It gives us a rule we can use, even before we know whether either condition is true in a particular case.</p>\\r\\n210:       <p className=\\"co-if-inline\\">We aren\'t saying the first condition happened. We\'re asking what would follow <strong>if</strong> it did.</p>\\r\\n211:       <HypotheticalFigure/>\\r\\n212:       <h3>Why learn this?</h3><p>On the LSAT, you need to distinguish what the information proves from what it leaves open. A conditional rule can help you prove a conclusion. An argument can also misuse the rule and reach a conclusion it hasn\'t proved. We\'ll practice telling those apart.</p>\\r\\n213:       <p>Let\'s read one rule.</p><blockquote>{raffleRule}</blockquote>\\r\\n214:       <p>Under this rule, winning the raffle makes getting a bicycle certain. Winning without getting one would break the stated relationship. That is what we mean by a <strong>guarantee</strong> here.</p>\\r\\n215:       <p>Compare that with “If you win the raffle, you usually get a bicycle.” Usually allows an exception. Our rule does not. Take the stated rule as given; we are asking what it tells us, not whether a real organizer keeps a promise.</p>\\r\\n216:       <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"Does the rule itself tell us that you actually win the raffle?\\" options={[\'Yes, it says I win.\', \'No, it tells me what follows if I win.\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'You added a case fact. The rule has not reported a win; it tells you what getting one would establish.\', \'Imagining a win helps us understand the rule. We need a separate fact to establish that you actually win.\']}/>\\r\\n217:       <p>Now let\'s take the two conditions one at a time.</p>\\r\\n218:     </ReferenceStep>\\r\\n219: \\r\\n220:     <ReferenceStep number={2} label=\\"Sufficient means enough\\" title=\\"One condition gives you enough\\">\\r\\n221:       <blockquote>{raffleRule}</blockquote><p>The <strong>if-part</strong> is the <strong>sufficient condition</strong>. Sufficient means enough. Knowing you win is enough, under this rule, to know you get a bicycle.</p>\\r\\n222:       <div className=\\"cr-role-exhibit\\"><span>Sufficient</span><strong>Win the raffle</strong><p>Enough to establish getting a bicycle</p></div>\\r\\n223:       <p>You might win. You might not. The rule has not settled that question.</p>\\r\\n224:       <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"You don\'t win the raffle. Could you still get a bicycle?\\" options={[\'Yes. Getting a bicycle is still possible.\', \'No. Without winning, getting a bicycle is impossible.\']} correct={0} feedback={[\'You could buy one or receive one as a gift. You could also not get one. Neither case creates a winner who fails to get a bicycle, so both fit the rule.\', \'You made winning the only way to get a bicycle. The sentence gives one sufficient condition; it has not excluded other ways.\']}/>\\r\\n225:       <p>Now suppose a fact gives us the sufficient condition. That is when we can <strong>use</strong>, or activate, the rule in its stated direction.</p>\\r\\n226:       <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"You win the raffle. Do we need another fact to establish that you get a bicycle?\\" options={[\'Yes, we need another fact about the bicycle.\', \'No, the rule and the win establish it.\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'The rule already covers the case in which you win. That fact meets its sufficient condition.\', \'Winning meets the sufficient condition. With the rule, that is enough to establish that you get a bicycle.\']}/>\\r\\n227:       <p>We\'ll now keep this job in mind while changing the wording.</p>\\r\\n228:     </ReferenceStep>\\r\\n229: \\r\\n"}\n{"chunk_id":"b308fe","wall_time_seconds":1.1229884,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":6680,"output":"import { Children, cloneElement, Fragment, isValidElement, useEffect, useId, useRef, useState, type ReactElement, type ReactNode } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { Fundamentals, GroupLessons } from \'./ConditionalOpeningLesson\';\\r\\nimport { conditionalReferenceContent, IncrementalWriter, IntegratedItem, referencePracticeItems, ReferenceQuestion, RuleDiagram, TargetReader, targetExamples, WordFamily } from \'./ConditionalReferenceSequence\';\\r\\nimport { CounterexampleLab, NegativeCaseLab, RuleComparisonLab } from \'./CounterexampleLab\';\\r\\nimport { MixedFamilyPractice } from \'./MixedFamilyPractice\';\\r\\nimport { OpeningActivityPractice } from \'./OpeningActivityPractice\';\\r\\nimport { StudyDeck } from \'./StudyDeck\';\\r\\n\\r\\ntype ContentSection = ReactElement<{ id?: string; number?: number; title?: string; children?: ReactNode; dark?: boolean; \'data-reference-boundary\'?: boolean }>;\\r\\n\\r\\n// Compose the approved source sections directly. Their stateful exercises are\\r\\n// mounted by React; the former reader-page renderer is never invoked here.\\r\\nfunction sections(children: ReactNode): ContentSection[] {\\r\\n  return Children.toArray(children).flatMap(child => {\\r\\n    if (!isValidElement<ContentSection[\'props\']>(child)) return [];\\r\\n    return child.type === Fragment ? sections(child.props.children) : [child];\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction reference(number: number): ContentSection {\\r\\n  const section = sections(conditionalReferenceContent().props.children).find(item => item.props.number === number);\\r\\n  if (!section) throw new Error(`Missing approved conditional reference section ${number}`);\\r\\n  return section;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction groupSections(group: \'group-1\' | \'group-2\') {\\r\\n  return sections(GroupLessons({ id: group }).props.children);\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction groupSection(group: \'group-1\' | \'group-2\', title: string) {\\r\\n  const section = groupSections(group).find(item => item.props.title === title);\\r\\n  if (!section) throw new Error(`Missing approved conditional section: ${title}`);\\r\\n  const sectionId = `course-${group}-${title.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \'-\')}`;\\r\\n  return cloneElement(section, { key: sectionId, id: sectionId });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction NoteSection({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }) {\\r\\n  return <section className=\\"co-section\\"><div className=\\"co-section-heading\\"><h2>{title}</h2></div>{children}</section>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction ReferenceBody({ number, omitTransition = false }: { number: number; omitTransition?: boolean }) {\\r\\n  const source = reference(number);\\r\\n  const children = Children.toArray(source.props.children);\\r\\n  // These source sections end in navigation prose for the former ordering.\\r\\n  // The course shell now owns transitions; the teaching stays in its source.\\r\\n  return <section className={`co-section cr-course-section ${source.props.dark ? \'cr-dark\' : \'\'}`} data-reference-source={number}>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-section-heading\\"><h2>{source.props.title}</h2></div>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"cr-step-body\\">{omitTransition ? children.slice(0, -1) : children}</div>\\r\\n  </section>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction FamilySupplement({ group }: { group: \'group-1\' | \'group-2\' }) {\\r\\n  const source = reference(group === \'group-1\' ? 3 : 5);\\r\\n  const family = sections(source.props.children).find(child => child.type === WordFamily);\\r\\n  if (!family) throw new Error(`Missing approved ${group} supplementary family`);\\r\\n  return <details className=\\"co-practical-not…1005 tokens truncated… === \'group-2\' && <OnlyAndOnlyIf/>}\\r\\n    <FamilySupplement group={group}/>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Recall the expressions\\"><p>Once you\'ve read the seven examples, use these cards to recall them. Explain the relationship before turning each card, then compare the complete ideas and the direction with your answer.</p><StudyDeck group={group}/></NoteSection>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// The raffle note sits before the families: arrow meaning, sides, one diagram and a moved if-clause.\\r\\nfunction MustArrowNote() {\\r\\n  return <section className=\\"co-section cr-course-section\\" data-reference-source={8}>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-section-heading\\"><h2>Give the relationship a shorter form</h2></div>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"cr-step-body\\">\\r\\n      <p>Put the sufficient condition on the <strong>left</strong>, the necessary condition on the <strong>right</strong>, and a <strong>must arrow</strong> between them.</p>\\r\\n      <RuleDiagram left=\\"Sufficient: enough\\" right=\\"Necessary: required\\"/>\\r\\n      <p>Read the arrow as <strong>must</strong>: if the left condition is true, the right condition must be true too. It does not mean the left caused the right, or that the right happens later.</p>\\r\\n      <p>The diagram holds the <strong>complete conditions</strong>, not the indicator word. An indicator helps you find a condition\'s job. Once you\'ve found that job, place the whole condition on its side of the arrow.</p>\\r\\n      <h3>Build the raffle diagram one step at a time</h3>\\r\\n      <p>First find the indicator, then draw the arrow, place the complete idea it refers to, and fill the other side.</p>\\r\\n      <IncrementalWriter/>\\r\\n      <h3>The sentence can move. The roles stay the same.</h3>\\r\\n      <p>Compare these two versions of our raffle rule. The indicator is bold; the complete idea it refers to is in brackets.</p>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cr-language-pair\\"><p><strong>If</strong> [you win the raffle], you get a bicycle.</p><p>You get a bicycle <strong>if</strong> [you win the raffle].</p><span>If identifies the sufficient condition in both sentences.</span></div>\\r\\n      <p>In the second sentence, if appears in the middle. Its condition comes last, but winning is still enough to establish getting a bicycle. Don\'t let the first phrase in English choose the first side of your diagram.</p>\\r\\n      <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"You get a bicycle if you win the raffle. Which diagram preserves this sentence?\\" options={[\'Get bicycle → Win\', \'Win → Get bicycle\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'You let sentence order choose the arrow. The if-clause still makes winning enough for getting a bicycle.\', \'The sufficient condition is still winning. Where it appears in the sentence does not change what it establishes.\']}/>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n  </section>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// One translation process, checked four ways, instead of five restarted lessons.\\r\\nfunction WholeMeaningNote() {\\r\\n  const onlyComparison = groupSection(\'group-2\', \'Compare only with the only\');\\r\\n  const insideCheck = Children.toArray(reference(12).props.children);\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Check a translation four ways\\">\\r\\n      <p>A diagram can point the right way and still say the wrong thing. Each check below catches a different slip in the same translation process.</p>\\r\\n      <ol>\\r\\n        <li><strong>Target:</strong> did you find the whole idea the indicator refers to, wherever it sits?</li>\\r\\n        <li><strong>Qualification:</strong> did your short label keep every restriction?</li>\\r\\n        <li><strong>Direction:</strong> did a similar-looking expression trick you into the wrong side?</li>\\r\\n        <li><strong>Imagined case:</strong> does your arrow promise something the sentence leaves open?</li>\\r\\n      </ol>\\r\\n    </NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Check 1: find the whole target, wherever it sits\\">\\r\\n      <p>An indicator\'s <strong>target</strong> is the complete word or phrase it refers to. In “All guests wearing blue badges receive vouchers,” all refers to guests wearing blue badges, not guests alone.</p>\\r\\n      <p>The target often comes after its indicator. But often isn\'t always. Try these sentences in order.</p>\\r\\n      {targetExamples.map(item => <TargetReader item={item} key={item.label}/>)}\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cr-language-pair\\"><p>[Having a key] is <strong>required</strong> for borrowing a locker.</p><p>Borrowing a locker <strong>requires</strong> [having a key].</p><span>Having a key is the requirement in both sentences.</span></div>\\r\\n      <p>The target comes before required in the first sentence and after requires in the second. Having a key still goes right. Neither the closest noun nor the next word is a reliable target rule. Ask which complete idea the sentence calls required, then give that idea its role.</p>\\r\\n    </NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Check 2: keep every qualification\\">\\r\\n      <p>Once you have the target, shorten it without losing what limits it. These two sentences test the same habit from opposite sides of the arrow.</p>\\r\\n      <div className=\\"co-word-comparison\\">\\r\\n        <div><blockquote>Any applicant who submits late pays a fee.</blockquote><p>Applicant alone isn\'t the condition. The sentence is about an applicant who submits late. Drop that restriction and your diagram makes a claim about on-time applicants too.</p></div>\\r\\n        <div><blockquote>Entry requires a current pass.</blockquote><p>A current pass is required. Has a pass drops current and would include an expired pass. A shorter label should save writing, not change the requirement.</p></div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n      <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"Any applicant who submits late pays a fee. What goes left?\\" options={[\'Applicant\', \'Applicant submits late\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'You dropped the late-submission restriction. Applicant alone would cover every applicant, including someone who submits on time. The sufficient idea is applicant submits late.\', \'This keeps the complete sufficient idea. The rule covers applicants who submit late, so keep that restriction with the applicant.\']}/>\\r\\n      <ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"Entry requires a current pass. What goes right?\\" options={[\'Has current pass\', \'Has any pass\']} correct={0} feedback={[\'This keeps the full requirement. Entry needs a current pass; an expired pass does not meet that requirement.\', \'You dropped current from the requirement. Has any pass would include an expired pass, which is not what the sentence requires.\']}/>\\r\\n    </NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Check 3: only and the only point to different targets\\">{onlyComparison.props.children}</NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Check 4: try the sentence from the inside\\">{insideCheck.slice(0, -1)}</NoteSection>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst freshCases = [\\r\\n  { rule: \'If it rains on Saturday, the market moves into the town hall.\', questions: [\\r\\n    { prompt: \\"It\'s Friday. You\'ve read this rule and nothing else. Where will the market be on Saturday?\\", options: [\'In the town hall.\', \'Outdoors, as usual.\', \\"The rule alone doesn\'t settle it.\\"], correct: 2, feedback: [\'You treated the rule as a weather report. It tells you what follows if it rains. Nobody has told you that it will rain.\', \\"You assumed it won\'t rain. The rule hasn\'t told you that either, so the market\'s location is still open.\\", \\"The rule describes what would happen if it rains. It\'s a promise about a possible case, not a report that the case happened.\\"] },\\r\\n    { prompt: \\"Saturday arrives, and it\'s raining. Now where is the market?\\", options: [\'In the town hall.\', \'Still not settled.\'], correct: 0, feedback: [\'Now you have a fact, not just a rule. Rain on Saturday meets the condition, so the rule puts the market in the town hall.\', \\"The fact changes things. The rule covers a rainy Saturday, and that\'s what we have, so the market\'s location is settled.\\"] },\\r\\n  ] },\\r\\n  { rule: \'Every student going on the museum trip has a signed permission slip.\', questions: [\\r\\n    { prompt: \'Which description matches this rule?\', options: [\'Going on the trip is enough to know a student has a signed slip.\', \'Having a signed slip is enough to know a student is going on the trip.\', \'Each one is enough to know the other.\'], correct: 0, feedback: [\'Pick any student on the trip: the rule says they have a signed slip. Going is enough to establish the slip, and the slip is what every student on the trip is required to have.\', \\"You made the requirement enough. A student could have a signed slip and stay at school with a cold. That student doesn\'t break the rule.\\", \\"The rule gives only one direction. Going on the trip establishes a signed slip. A student with a slip who stays behind fits the rule, so the slip doesn\'t establish going.\\"] },\\r\\n  ] },\\r\\n  { rule: \'If the café runs out of bread, it closes early.\', questions: [\\r\\n    { prompt: \'Today the café did not run out of bread. Did it close early?\', options: [\'No. It stayed open until the usual time.\', \'Yes. It closed early.\', \'Not settled. It could stay open or close early for another reason.\'], correct: 2, feedback: [\'You treated running out of bread as the only reason to close early. The rule gives one reason. A café that closed early because of a power cut would still fit it.\', \\"Nothing here establishes an early closing. The rule tells us what happens when the bread runs out, and today it didn\'t.\\", \\"Given that fact, the rule doesn\'t settle whether the café closed early. It could have stayed open, or closed early for another reason. Both cases fit the rule.\\"] },\\r\\n  ] },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction FoundationPractice() {\\r\\n  const anchor = sections(reference(1).props.children).find(child => child.type === ReferenceQuestion);\\r\\n  if (!anchor) throw new Error(\'Missing approved raffle retrieval question\');\\r\\n  const fresh = sections(Fundamentals().props.children).find(section => section.props.title === \'Enough does not mean the only way\');\\r\\n  if (!fresh) throw new Error(\'Missing approved fresh sufficient-condition check\');\\r\\n  const freshContent = Children.toArray(fresh.props.children);\\r\\n  const freshCheck = freshContent[freshContent.length - 1];\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Separate the rule from the fact\\"><blockquote>If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.</blockquote><p>Start with the rule you know. Decide what the rule itself establishes before any fact arrives.</p>{anchor}</NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Carry the ideas to new rules\\">\\r\\n      <p>Now the situations change. Take each rule as given and decide what it establishes, what it requires and what it leaves open. A wrong answer is a reason to inspect the connection and try again.</p>\\r\\n      {freshCases.map(item => <div key={item.rule} className=\\"co-fresh-case\\"><blockquote>{item.rule}</blockquote>{item.questions.map(question => <ReferenceQuestion key={question.prompt} {...question}/>)}</div>)}\\r\\n    </NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Try a fresh case\\">{freshCheck}</NoteSection>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Test the limit with a possible case\\"><ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"Someone claims that getting a bicycle proves you won the raffle. Which possible case fits our rule but disproves that claim?\\" options={[\'You win and get a bicycle.\', \'You do not win, but get a bicycle as a gift.\', \'You win and never get a bicycle.\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'This fits both the rule and the claim. It does not show why the claim adds something.\', \'The rule promises a bicycle to a winner. It does not exclude a bicycle received as a gift without winning, so this case fits the rule and disproves the claim.\', \'Winning without getting a bicycle breaks the original rule. We need a case that keeps the rule true.\']}/></NoteSection>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction GroupPractice({ group }: { group: \'group-1\' | \'group-2\' }) {\\r\\n  const meaningCheck = groupSection(group, \'Check the meaning, not just the word\');\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    {groupSection(group, group === \'group-1\' ? \'Find the target, then place it\' : \'Find the requirement, then place it\')}\\r\\n    {groupSection(group, \'From sentence to diagram\')}\\r\\n    {group === \'group-1\' && groupSection(group, \'Read the only as a complete expression\')}\\r\\n    {cloneElement(meaningCheck, {}, Children.toArray(meaningCheck.props.children).slice(0, -1))}\\r\\n    <details className=\\"co-practical-note\\"><summary>More sentences to recall and explain</summary>{groupSection(group, \'Say the rule without the clues\')}</details>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction PermissionBoundary() {\\r\\n  const boundary = sections(conditionalReferenceContent().props.children).find(section => section.props[\'data-reference-boundary\'] !== undefined);\\r\\n  if (!boundary) throw new Error(\'Missing approved permission and instruction boundary\');\\r\\n  return <NoteSection title=\\"Keep permission and instructions distinct\\"><p>A conditional can give permission or an instruction. Preserve that strength instead of turning it into a report of what someone did.</p>{Children.toArray(boundary.props.children).slice(3)}</NoteSection>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction CompatibleCasesRecap() {\\r\\n  return <NoteSection title=\\"Three combinations fit. One breaks the rule.\\">\\r\\n    <blockquote>If you win the raffle, you get a bicycle.</blockquote>\\r\\n    <p>The rule requires a bicycle when you win. Let\'s compare all four possible combinations. These are possible cases, not a claim that any of them happened.</p>\\r\\n    <table className=\\"co-combination-table\\">\\r\\n      <caption>Possible cases under the raffle rule</caption>\\r\\n      <thead><tr><th scope=\\"col\\">Win the raffle?</th><th scope=\\"col\\">Get a bicycle?</th><th scope=\\"col\\">Fits the rule?</th></tr></thead>\\r\\n      <tbody>\\r\\n        <tr><td>Yes</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes. The required result is present.</td></tr>\\r\\n        <tr><td>No</td><td>Yes</td><td>Yes. The rule allows a bicycle without a win.</td></tr>\\r\\n        <tr><td>No</td><td>No</td><td>Yes. With no win, the rule settles nothing about a bicycle.</td></tr>\\r\\n        <tr><td>Yes</td><td>No</td><td><strong>No. This is the single violation.</strong></td></tr>\\r\\n      </tbody>\\r\\n    </table>\\r\\n    <p>A conditional is broken when its sufficient condition is present and its necessary condition is absent. That\'s why no bicycle rules out a win: a winner without a bicycle would be the forbidden combination.</p>\\r\\n  </NoteSection>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction MixedPracticeRounds() {\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    <NoteSection title=\\"Work through three rounds\\">\\r\\n      <p>First practice the necessary expressions. Then translate fresh sentences from both families. Finish by comparing different ways to state one rule.</p>\\r\\n      <nav className=\\"co-round-links\\" aria-label=\\"Practice rounds\\">\\r\\n        <a href=\\"#necessary-round\\">1. Necessary-condition practice</a>\\r\\n        <a href=\\"#translation-round\\">2. Mixed translation</a>\\r\\n        <a href=\\"#comparison-round\\">3. Equivalent-rule comparison</a>\\r\\n      </nav>\\r\\n    </NoteSection>\\r\\n    <section className=\\"co-practice-round\\" id=\\"necessary-round\\" aria-labelledby=\\"necessary-round-title\\">\\r\\n      <h2 className=\\"co-round-heading\\" id=\\"necessary-round-title\\">Round 1: Necessary-condition practice</h2>\\r\\n      <GroupPractice group=\\"group-2\\"/>\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n    <section className=\\"co-practice-round\\" id=\\"translation-round\\" aria-labelledby=\\"translation-round-title\\">\\r\\n      <h2 className=\\"co-round-heading\\" id=\\"translation-round-title\\">Round 2: Mixed translation</h2>\\r\\n      <NoteSection title=\\"Translate before looking at the condition labels\\"><MixedFamilyPractice/></NoteSection>\\r\\n      <NoteSection title=\\"Try seven more sentences\\"><OpeningActivityPractice/></NoteSection>\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n    <section className=\\"co-practice-round\\" id=\\"comparison-round\\" aria-labelledby=\\"comparison-round-title\\">\\r\\n      <h2 className=\\"co-round-heading\\" id=\\"comparison-round-title\\">Round 3: Equivalent-rule comparison</h2>\\r\\n      <NoteSection title=\\"Keep the rule while the wording changes\\"><RuleComparisonLab/></NoteSection>\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction ContrapositivePractice() {\\r\\n  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0);\\r\\n  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<boolean[]>(() => referencePracticeItems.map(() => false));\\r\\n  return <NoteSection title=\\"Build an original and its contrapositive\\">\\r\\n    <p>For each sentence, identify the indicator and both complete conditions. Choose the original diagram, then its contrapositive. Read both back before checking. If an answer needs revision, inspect the direction, qualifications and negation, then retry.</p>\\r\\n    <p className=\\"co-kicker\\" role=\\"status\\">Sentence {current + 1} of {referencePracticeItems.length}</p>\\r\\n    {referencePracticeItems.map((item, index) => <div key={item.sentence} hidden={current !== index} data-course-practice-item={index + 1}><IntegratedItem item={item} number={index + 1} onChecked={value => setChecked(previous => previous.map((old, itemIndex) => itemIndex === index ? value : old))}/></div>)}\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-choices\\"><button type=\\"button\\" disabled={current === 0} onClick={() => setCurrent(current - 1)}>Previous sentence</button><button type=\\"button\\" disabled={current === referencePracticeItems.length - 1 || !checked[current]} onClick={() => setCurrent(current + 1)}>Next sentence</button></div>\\r\\n    <p>Moving between sentences keeps your choices and feedback.</p>\\r\\n  </NoteSection>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction Content({ id }: { id: string }) {\\r\\n  switch (id) {\\r\\n    case \'meaning\': return <ReferenceBody number={1} omitTransition/>;\\r\\n    case \'sufficient\': return <ReferenceBody number={2} omitTransition/>;\\r\\n    case \'necessary\': return <><ReferenceBody number={4} omitTransition/><ReferenceQuestion prompt=\\"You get a bicycle. Does that establish that you won the raffle?\\" options={[\'Yes. Getting a bicycle proves winning.\', \'No. You could get a bicycle without winning.\']} correct={1} feedback={[\'You made the required result enough to establish winning. The rule does not say winning is the only way to get a bicycle.\', \'You could buy a bicycle or receive one as a gift. Neither possibility breaks the rule, which only tells us what must follow if you win.\']}/></>;\\r\\n    case \'must-arrow\': return <><MustArrowNote/><NoteSection title=\\"Recall enough and required\\"><p>Use these new examples to check the roles and their direction.</p><StudyDeck group=\\"foundation\\"/></NoteSection></>;\\r\\n    case \'sufficient-words\': return <IndicatorNote group=\\"group-1\\"/>;\\r\\n    case \'necessary-words\': return <><IndicatorNote group=\\"group-2\\"/><PermissionBoundary/></>;\\r\\n    case \'whole-meaning\': return <WholeMeaningNote/>;\\r\\n    case \'missing-requirement\': return <><ReferenceBody number={6}/><ReferenceBody number={7} omitTransition/></>;\\r\\n    case \'contrapositive\': return <><ReferenceBody number={9}/><details className=\\"co-practical-note\\"><summary>Practical questions about diagramming</summary><ReferenceBody number={10} omitTransition/></details><NoteSection title=\\"Two possible rooms, one limit\\"><NegativeCaseLab/></NoteSection><CompatibleCasesRecap/></>;\\r\\n    case \'foundation-practice\': return <FoundationPractice/>;\\r\\n    case \'sufficient-practice\': return <><GroupPractice group=\\"group-1\\"/><NoteSection title=\\"Build a case that proves the limit\\"><CounterexampleLab/></NoteSection></>;\\r\\n    case \'mixed-practice\': return <MixedPracticeRounds/>;\\r\\n    case \'contrapositive-practice\': return <ContrapositivePractice/>;\\r\\n    default: throw new Error(`Unknown opening course content: ${id}`);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Article body only. Course routes own titles, navigation and completion. */\\r\\nexport function OpeningCourseContent({ id }: { id: string }) {\\r\\n  return <div className=\\"co-opening cr-sequence opening-course-content\\"><Content key={id} id={id}/></div>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

What goes right?\' })).toBeVisible();\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\nit(\'makes all three assigned practice rounds visible and links to their working content\', () => {\\r\\n  render(<OpeningCourseContent id=\\"mixed-practice\\"/>);\\r\\n  const rounds = [\\r\\n    [\'Round 1: Necessary-condition practice\', \'necessary-round\'],\\r\\n    [\'Round 2: Mixed translation\', \'translation-round\'],\\r\\n    [\'Round 3: Equivalent-rule comparison\', \'comparison-round\'],\\r\\n  ];\\r\\n  for (const [heading, id] of rounds) {\\r\\n    expect(screen.getByRole(\'heading\', { name: heading })).toBeVisible();\\r\\n    const round = document.getElementById(id)!;\\r\\n    expect(round).toBeVisible();\\r\\n    expect(round.querySelector(\'button\')).toBeTruthy();\\r\\n    expect(within(screen.getByRole(\'navigation\', { name: \'Practice rounds\' })).getAllByRole(\'link\').find(link => link.getAttribute(\'href\') === `#${id}`)).toBeTruthy();\\r\\n  }\\r\\n});\\r\\n\\r\\nit(\'shows every combination of the two conditions and rejects only a win without a bicycle\', () => {\\r\\n  render(<OpeningCourseContent id=\\"contrapositive\\"/>);\\r\\n  const recap = section(\'Three combinations fit. Leaving out wearing a gold badge would make a claim about all guests.\', instruction: \'Choose the description that keeps the restriction in each sentence.\' },\\r\\n  { title: \'Build the diagram\', purpose: \'If the condition on the left is true, what must follow on the right?\', example: \'If a parcel has a blue sticker, it has been inspected.\', explanation: \'A blue sticker is enough to establish inspection. Read the diagram in English before checking.\' },\\r\\n  { title: \'Translate the sentence yourself\', purpose: \'Write your own diagram before looking at supplied conditions.\', example: \'Only members use the studio.\', explanation: \'First decide what the sentence says you need in order to use the studio. Compare with the choices only after making that attempt.\' },\\r\\n  { title: \'Does the meaning change?\', purpose: \'Different wording can preserve a rule or change what it promises.\', example: \'Every parcel in Locker 4 has been scanned.\', explanation: \'This tells you something about parcels in Locker 4. Try a possible case that fits one but would break the other.\' },\\r\\n  { title: \'Try unfamiliar cases\', purpose: \'Use the same reasoning with new wording.\', example: \'Visitors must wear a badge.\', explanation: \'This gives visitors an obligation. Keep qualifications and the strength of the claim.\' },\\r\\n  { title: \'Try the distinction in a new case\', purpose: \'Now use the distinctions you revised with different sentences.\', example: \'\', explanation: \'The next questions revisit mistakes from this practice with fresh examples.\', instruction: \'Make a new attempt before checking. An answer you reach after feedback is useful practice, but it is different from getting a fresh case right on the first check.\' },\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\ntype Question =\\r\\n  | { id: string; stage: number; kind: \'choice\'; item: MixedDrillChoiceItem }\\r\\n  | { id: string; stage: number; kind: \'diagram\'; item: (typeof openingActivityItems)[number] }\\r\\n  | { id: string; stage: number; kind: \'independent\'; item: (typeof mixedFamilyItems)[number]; itemIndex: number };\\r\\n\\r\\nconst baselineQuestions: Question[] = [\\r\\n  ...completeConditionItems.map(item => ({ id: item.id, stage: 0, kind: \'choice\' as const, item })),\\r\\n  ...openingActivityItems.map(item => ({ id: item.id, stage: 1, kind: \'diagram\' as const, item })),\\r\\n  ...mixedFamilyItems.map((item, itemIndex) => ({ id: `mixed-independent-${itemIndex + 1}`, stage: 2, kind: \'independent\' as const, item, itemIndex })),\\r\\n  ...equivalenceItems.map(item => ({ id: item.id, stage: 3, kind: \'choice\' as const, item })),\\r\\n  ...transferItems.map(item => ({ id: item.id, stage: 4, kind: \'choice\' as const, item })),\\r\\n];\\r\\n\\r\\nconst errorLabels: Record<MixedDrillError, string> = {\\r\\n  qualification: \'Who or what the rule covers\', direction: \'Reading the arrow in the right direction\',\\r\\n  requirement: \'Separating what is enough from what is required\', equivalence: \'Comparing the whole meaning\',\\r\\n};\\r\\ntype Result = { firstCorrect: boolean; correct: boolean; errors: MixedDrillError[] };\\r\\ntype Check = (correct: boolean | null, errors?: MixedDrillError[]) => void;\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction confirmationQuestions(results: Record<string, Result>): Question[] {\\r\\n  const errors = [...new Set(baselineQuestions.flatMap(item => results[item.id]?.errors ?? []))];\\r\\n  return errors.flatMap(error => {\\r\\n    const items = error === \'equivalence\' && results[\'mixed-transfer-4\']?.errors.includes(\'equivalence\') ? [obligationConfirmationItem] : confirmationItemsByError[error].slice(0, 1);\\r\\n    return items.map(item => ({ id: item.id, stage: 5, kind: \'choice\' as const, item }));\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction ChoiceQuestion({ item, onCheck }: { item: MixedDrillChoiceItem; onCheck: Check }) {\\r\\n  const [picked, setPicked] = useState<number | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);\\r\\n  return <div className=\\"omd-question\\">\\r\\n    <blockquote>{item.sentence}</blockquote><h3>{item.prompt}</h3>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"omd-options\\" role=\\"group\\" aria-label=\\"Answer choices\\">{item.options.map((option, index) => <button type=\\"button\\" key={option} aria-pressed={picked === index} onClick={() => { setPicked(index); setChecked(false); onCheck(null); }}>{option}</button>)}</div>\\r\\n    <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"omd-primary\\" disabled={picked === null} onClick={() => { setChecked(true); onCheck(picked === item.correct, picked === item.correct ? [] : [item.errorByOption?.[picked] ?? item.skill]); }}>Check answer</button>\\r\\n    {checked && <div role=\\"status\\" className=\\"omd-feedback\\" data-correct={picked === item.correct}><p>{item.feedback[picked]}</p>\\r\\n      {picked !== item.correct && <button type=\\"button\\" onClick={() => { setChecked(false); setPicked(null); onCheck(null); }}>Try this question again</button>}\\r\\n    </div>}\\r\\n  </div>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction DrillQuestion({ question, onCheck }: { question: Question; onCheck: Check }) {\\r\\n  if (question.kind === \'choice\') return <ChoiceQuestion item={question.item} onCheck={onCheck}/>;\\r\\n  if (question.kind === \'independent\') return <MixedItemCheck item={question.item} index={question.itemIndex} embedded onChecked={() => undefined} onResult={result => {\\r\\n    if (!result) return onCheck(null);\\r\\n    const errors: MixedDrillError[] = [];\\r\\n    if (!result.targetCorrect) errors.push(result.targetIncomplete ? \'qualification\' : \'direction\');\\r\\n    if (!result.sideCorrect) errors.push(question.item.family === \'necessary\' ? \'requirement\' : \'direction\');\\r\\n    onCheck(result.targetCorrect && result.sideCorrect, errors);\\r\\n  }}/>;\\r\\n  return <div className=\\"cir-root omd-diagram\\"><p>Build the original diagram, then read it as an if/then sentence before checking.</p>\\r\\n    <DiagramDrills items={[question.item]} embedded persistProgress={false} allowContrapositive={false} allowNegation={false} shuffleItems={false} explanations={openingActivityExplanations} feedbackForResult={result => {\\r\\n      if (result.correct) return `Your diagram keeps ${question.item.left} as sufficient for ${question.item.right}.`;\\r\\n      const reversed = result.left === question.item.right && result.right === question.item.left;\\r\\n      if (reversed) return `Your arrow makes ${question.item.right} enough to establish ${question.item.left}. The rule makes ${question.item.left} enough to establish ${question.item.right}, and leaves the reverse open.`;\\r\\n      return `Your diagram has “${result.left}” on the left and “${result.right}” on the right. Use each condition once: ${question.item.left} on the left, ${question.item.right} on the right. Extra or repeated words change the diagram.`;\\r\\n    }} onResult={result => {\\r\\n      if (!result) return onCheck(null);\\r\\n      const reversed = result.left === question.item.right && result.right === question.item.left;\\r\\n      onCheck(result.correct, result.correct ? [] : [reversed && question.item.group === \'g2\' ? \'requirement\' : \'direction\']);\\r\\n    }}/>\\r\\n  </div>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function OpeningMixedDrill() {\\r\\n  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState<Question[]>(baselineQuestions);\\r\\n  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0);\\r\\n  const [visited, setVisited] = useState([baselineQuestions[0].id]);\\r\\n  const [results, setResults] = useState<Record<string, Result>>({});\\r\\n  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const [introducedStages, setIntroducedStages] = useState<number[]>([]);\\r\\n  const heading = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);\\r\\n  const runner = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);\\r\\n  const question = questions[current];\\r\\n  const stage = stages[question.stage];\\r\\n  const showIntroduction = !finished && !introducedStages.includes(question.stage);\\r\\n  const lastPosition = useRef({ current, finished, showIntroduction });\\r\\n  useEffect(() => {\\r\\n    if (lastPosition.current.current === current && lastPosition.current.finished === finished && lastPosition.current.showIntroduction === showIntroduction) return;\\r\\n    lastPosition.current = { current, finished, showIntroduction };\\r\\n    heading.current?.focus({ preventScroll: true });\\r\\n    runner.current?.scrollIntoView?.({ block: \'start\', behavior: \'instant\' });\\r\\n  }, [current, finished, showIntroduction]);\\r\\n\\r\\n  function record(id: string, correct: boolean | null, errors: MixedDrillError[] = []) {\\r\\n    setResults(previous => {\\r\\n      const old = previous[id];\\r\\n      if (correct === null) return old ? { ...previous, [id]: { ...old, correct: false } } : previous;\\r\\n      return { ...previous, [id]: old ? { ...old, correct, errors: [...new Set([...old.errors, ...errors])] } : { firstCorrect: correct, correct, errors } };\\r\\n    });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  function move(index: number) {\\r\\n    setCurrent(index);\\r\\n    setVisited(previous => previous.includes(questions[index].id) ? previous : [...previous, questions[index].id]);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const pendingConfirmations = questions.length === baselineQuestions.length ? confirmationQuestions(results) : [];\\r\\n  function next() {\\r\\n    if (current + 1 < questions.length) return move(current + 1);\\r\\n    if (questions.length === baselineQuestions.length) {\\r\\n      const confirmations = pendingConfirmations;\\r\\n      if (confirmations.length) {\\r\\n        setQuestions(previous => [...previous, ...confirmations]);\\r\\n        setVisited(previous => [...previous, confirmations[0].id]);\\r\\n        setCurrent(current + 1);\\r\\n        return;\\r\\n      }\\r\\n    }\\r\\n    setFinished(true);\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const firstCorrect = baselineQuestions.filter(item => results[item.id]?.firstCorrect).length;\\r\\n  const corrected = baselineQuestions.filter(item => results[item.id]?.firstCorrect === false && results[item.id]?.correct).length;\\r\\n  const confirmations = questions.filter(item => item.stage === 5);\\r\\n  return <section ref={runner} className=\\"omd-runner\\" aria-label=\\"Conditional translation practice\\">\\r\\n    <div className=\\"omd-summary\\" hidden={!finished}><h2 ref={finished ? heading : undefined} tabIndex={-1}>Review your practice</h2>\\r\\n      <p>You answered {firstCorrect} of 24 correctly on your first try. You corrected {corrected} after checking the explanation.</p>\\r\\n      <p>This result covers your answer choices and the diagrams you built with the supplied words. Your written translations are for you to compare with the examples.</p>\\r\\n      {!!confirmations.length && <><h3>How the new sentences went</h3><ul>{confirmations.map(item => <li key={item.id}>{item.id === obligationConfirmationItem.id ? \'What is required and what actually happened\' : item.kind === \'choice\' && errorLabels[item.item.skill]}: {results[item.id]?.firstCorrect ? \'you answered the new question correctly on your first try\' : \'you corrected the new question after checking the explanation. Give this another try with a different sentence\'}.</li>)}</ul></>}\\r\\n      <p>When you return to a sentence, read your diagram back in English. Keep every restriction, and ask whether your arrow promises more than the sentence does.</p>\\r\\n      <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"omd-primary\\" onClick={() => { setFinished(false); move(0); }}>Review my answers</button>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"omd-introduction\\" hidden={!showIntroduction}>\\r\\n      <h2 ref={showIntroduction ? heading : undefined} tabIndex={-1}>{stage.title}</h2>\\r\\n      <p>{stage.purpose}</p>\\r\\n      {stage.example && <blockquote>{stage.example}</blockquote>}\\r\\n      <p>{stage.explanation}</p><p>{stage.instruction}</p>\\r\\n      <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"omd-primary\\" onClick={() => setIntroducedStages(previous => [...previous, question.stage])}>{question.stage === 0 ? \'Begin practice\' : \'Continue\'}</button>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n    <div hidden={finished || showIntroduction}>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"omd-progress-heading\\"><p>{question.stage < 5 ? `Stage ${question.stage + 1} of 5` : \'24 main questions completed\'}</p><p>{question.stage < 5 ? `Question ${current + 1} of 24` : `Fresh check ${current - 23} of ${questions.length - 24}`}</p></div>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"omd-progress\\" role=\\"progressbar\\" aria-label={question.stage < 5 ? \'Main questions completed\' : \'Fresh checks completed\'} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={question.stage < 5 ? 24 : questions.length - 24} aria-valuenow={questions.filter(item => (item.stage === 5) === (question.stage === 5) && results[item.id]?.correct).length}><span style={{ width: `${questions.filter(item => (item.stage === 5) === (question.stage === 5) && results[item.id]?.correct).length / (question.stage < 5 ? 24 : questions.length - 24) * 100}%` }}/></div>\\r\\n    <h2 ref={!finished && !showIntroduction ? heading : undefined} tabIndex={-1}>{stage.title}</h2><p className=\\"omd-purpose\\">{stage.purpose}</p>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n    {questions.filter(item => visited.includes(item.id)).map(item => <div key={item.id} hidden={finished || showIntroduction || item.id !== question.id} data-mixed-question={item.id}><DrillQuestion question={item} onCheck={(correct, errors) => record(item.id, correct, errors)}/></div>)}\\r\\n    {!finished && !showIntroduction && <nav className=\\"omd-navigation\\" aria-label=\\"Practice questions\\"><button type=\\"button\\" disabled={current === 0} onClick={() => move(current - 1)}>Previous question</button><button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"omd-primary\\" disabled={!results[question.id]?.correct} onClick={next}>{current === questions.length - 1 ? (question.stage === 5 ? \'Finish practice\' : pendingConfirmations.length ? \'Try a fresh case\' : \'Review practice\') : \'Next question\'}</button></nav>}\\r\\n  </section>;\\r\\n}\\r\\nimport { useId, useRef, useState } from \'react\';\\r\\nimport { RuleDiagram } from \'./ConditionalReferenceSequence\';\\r\\n\\r\\ntype MixedItem = {\\r\\n  sentence: string;\\r\\n  word: string;\\r\\n  family: \'sufficient\' | \'necessary\';\\r\\n  target: string;\\r\\n  targetPrompt: string;\\r\\n  targetFeedback: string;\\r\\n  partial: string;\\r\\n  other: string;\\r\\n  left: string;\\r\\n  right: string;\\r\\n  partialFeedback: string;\\r\\n  otherFeedback: string;\\r\\n  reversed: string;\\r\\n  why: string;\\r\\n  readback: string;\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\n// Original sentences. The families alternate and no label is shown until the\\r\\n// learner has checked a translation. Do not promise an unpredictable order.\\r\\nexport const mixedFamilyItems: MixedItem[] = [\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    sentence: \'The match moves indoors if the forecast shows lightning.\', word: \'if\', family: \'sufficient\',\\r\\n    targetPrompt: \'What does the forecast have to show for this rule to establish a move indoors?\', targetFeedback: \'You identified the forecast showing lightning.\',\\r\\n    target: \'the forecast shows lightning\', partial: \'the forecast\', other: \'the match moves indoors\',\\r\\n    left: \'Forecast shows lightning\', right: \'Match moves indoors\',\\r\\n    partialFeedback: \\"The forecast alone isn\'t something that can be true or false. If refers to the whole clause: the forecast shows lightning.\\",\\r\\n    otherFeedback: \'Moving indoors is what the rule establishes. If points to the clause after it: the forecast shows lightning.\',\\r\\n    reversed: \'Your arrow makes every indoor match proof of a lightning forecast. The match could move indoors because the pitch is flooded, and that case still fits the sentence.\',\\r\\n    why: \\"The if-clause comes last, but it\'s still the part that\'s enough. A lightning forecast establishes the move indoors.\\",\\r\\n    readback: \'If the forecast shows lightning, the match moves indoors.\',\\r\\n  },\\r\\n  {\\r\\n    sentence: \'A student joins the choir trip only if a parent signs the consent form.\', word: \'only if\', family: \'necessary\',\\r\\n    targetPrompt: \'What does a student need in order to join the choir trip?\', targetFeedback: \'You kept what the parent must do: sign the consent form.\',\\r\\n    target: \'a parent signs the consent form\', partial: \'a parent\', other: \'a student joins the choir trip\',\\r\\n    left: \'Joins choir trip\', right: \'Parent signs consent form\',\\r\\n    partialFeedback: \'A parent alone drops what the parent has to do. The sentence requires a booking, not just any table.\\",\\r\\n    target: \'a booked table\', partial: \'a table\', other: \'eat on the terrace\',\\r\\n    left: \'Eats on terrace\', right: \'Has booked table\',\\r\\n    partialFeedback: \\"A table drops booked, and booked is the requirement. [item.right, item.left] : [item.left, item.right];\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function MixedItemCheck({ item, index, onChecked, onResult, embedded = false }: { item: MixedItem; index: number; onChecked: (checked: boolean) => void; onResult?: (result: { targetCorrect: boolean; targetIncomplete: boolean; sideCorrect: boolean } | null) => void; embedded?: boolean }) {\\r\\n  const [draftDiagram, setDraftDiagram] = useState(\'\');\\r\\n  const [draftReadback, setDraftReadback] = useState(\'\');\\r\\n  const [comparing, setComparing] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const draftQuestion = useId();\\r\\n  const readbackQuestion = useId();\\r\\n  const draftInput = useRef<HTMLTextAreaElement>(null);\\r\\n  const [target, setTarget] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [left, setLeft] = useState<string | null>(null);\\r\\n  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const targetQuestion = useId();\\r\\n  const sideQuestion = useId();\\r\\n  const change = (update: () => void) => { update(); setChecked(false); onChecked(false); onResult?.(null); };\\r\\n  const reset = () => { draftInput.current?.focus(); setComparing(false); setTarget(null); setLeft(null); setChecked(false); onChecked(false); onResult?.(null); };\\r\\n  const targetCorrect = target === item.target;\\r\\n  const sideCorrect = left === item.left;\\r\\n  const number = index + 1;\\r\\n  return <div className=\\"co-lab co-check\\" data-mixed-item={number}>\\r\\n    <blockquote>{item.sentence}</blockquote>\\r\\n    <p>Write the complete conditions and the arrow yourself. Then read your diagram back as an if/then sentence. You can use words or short labels you define.</p>\\r\\n    <label htmlFor={draftQuestion}>{embedded ? \'Your diagram\' : `Your diagram for sentence ${number}`}</label>\\r\\n    <textarea ref={draftInput} id={draftQuestion} className=\\"co-attempt-input\\" rows={3} value={draftDiagram} readOnly={comparing} onChange={event => setDraftDiagram(event.target.value)}/>\\r\\n    <label htmlFor={readbackQuestion}>{embedded ? \'Read it in English\' : `Your English readback for sentence ${number}`}</label>\\r\\n    <textarea id={readbackQuestion} className=\\"co-attempt-input\\" rows={3} value={draftReadback} readOnly={comparing} onChange={event => setDraftReadback(event.target.value)}/>\\r\\n    {!comparing && <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"co-primary\\" disabled={!draftDiagram.trim() || !draftReadback.trim()} onClick={() => setComparing(true)}>{embedded ? \'Compare with the choices\' : `Compare my translation for sentence ${number}`}</button>}\\r\\n    {comparing && <>\\r\\n    <p>{embedded ? \\"Compare your written diagram with these choices. Check that you kept the complete conditions and the arrow\'s direction.\\" : \'Your written answer is saved for you to compare. The choices below check who or what the rule covers and the arrow direction; your written answer is not graded automatically.\'}</p>\\r\\n    <h3 id={targetQuestion}>{item.targetPrompt}</h3>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-choices\\" role=\\"group\\" aria-labelledby={targetQuestion}>{targetOptions(item, index).map(option => <button type=\\"button\\" key={option} aria-pressed={target === option} onClick={() => change(() => setTarget(option))}>{option}</button>)}</div>\\r\\n    <h3 id={sideQuestion}>Which condition gives you enough to establish the other?</h3>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-choices\\" role=\\"group\\" aria-labelledby={sideQuestion}>{sideOptions(item, index).map(option => <button type=\\"button\\" key={option} aria-pressed={left === option} onClick={() => change(() => setLeft(option))}>{option}</button>)}</div>\\r\\n    {left && <RuleDiagram left={left} right={left === item.left ? item.right : item.left} label={`Your diagram: ${left} implies ${left === item.left ? item.right : item.left}`}/>}\\r\\n    <div className=\\"cr-control-row\\"><button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"co-primary\\" disabled={target === null || left === null} onClick={() => { setChecked(true); onChecked(true); onResult?.({ targetCorrect, targetIncomplete: target === item.partial, sideCorrect }); }}>{embedded ? \'Check answer\' : `Check sentence ${number}`}</button><button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cr-reset\\" onClick={reset}>{embedded ? \'Revise my answer\' : `Try sentence ${number} again`}</button></div>\\r\\n    <div role=\\"status\\">{checked && <div className={`co-feedback ${targetCorrect && sideCorrect ? \'co-correct\' : \'\'}`}>\\r\\n      {targetCorrect && sideCorrect ? <>\\r\\n        <strong>Read it back: {item.readback}</strong>\\r\\n        <p>{item.why}</p>\\r\\n      </> : <>\\r\\n      <strong>Revise the part described below.</strong>\\r\\n      <p>{targetCorrect ? item.targetFeedback : target === item.partial ? item.partialFeedback : item.otherFeedback}</p>\\r\\n      <p>{sideCorrect ? \'Your arrow keeps the direction the sentence gives.\' : item.reversed}</p>\\r\\n      <p>Here, <strong>{item.word}</strong> points to what is {item.family === \'sufficient\' ? \'enough, so its target goes on the left\' : \'required, so its target goes on the right\'}. {item.why}</p>\\r\\n      <p>Read it back: {item.readback}</p>\\r\\n      </>}\\r\\n    </div>}</div>\\r\\n    </>}\\r\\n    {!comparing && <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cr-reset\\" onClick={reset}>{embedded ? \'Revise my answer\' : `Try sentence ${number} again`}</button>}\\r\\n  </div>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function MixedFamilyPractice() {\\r\\n  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0);\\r\\n  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<boolean[]>(() => mixedFamilyItems.map(() => false));\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    <p>These sentences use both kinds of expression. The family name stays hidden until you check your choices. First translate each sentence yourself, without supplied condition labels. Then compare your work and check who or what the rule covers and the arrow direction before moving on.</p>\\r\\n    <p className=\\"co-kicker\\" role=\\"status\\">Sentence {current + 1} of {mixedFamilyItems.length}</p>\\r\\n    {mixedFamilyItems.map((item, index) => <div key={item.sentence} hidden={current !== index}><MixedItemCheck item={item} index={index} onChecked={value => setChecked(previous => previous.map((old, itemIndex) => itemIndex === index ? value : old))}/></div>)}\\r\\n    <div className=\\"co-choices\\"><button type=\\"button\\" disabled={current === 0} onClick={() => setCurrent(current - 1)}>Previous mixed sentence</button><button type=\\"button\\" disabled={current === mixedFamilyItems.length - 1 || !checked[current]} onClick={() => setCurrent(current + 1)}>Next mixed sentence</button></div>\\r\\n    <p>Moving between sentences keeps your written attempt, choices, and feedback. Trying a sentence again keeps your writing so you can revise it, and hides the supplied labels until you compare again.</p>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.0 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"f3785d","wall_time_seconds":0.3444062,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1619,"output":"# Conditional drill: polish followed by a fresh audit, 2026-10-07\\r\\n\\r\\n## The drill is clearer; one small readability refinement remains\\r\\n\\r\\nAnti-pattern verdict: pass for the scoped drill. One task is shown at a time, each stage has a separate briefing, each exercise uses one panel, and the prompts ask about the actual sentence. This is a source and rendered-interface judgment, not a claim that student learning outcomes have been measured.\\r\\n\\r\\n| Dimension | Score | Evidence and limits |\\r\\n|---|---:|---|\\r\\n| Accessibility | 3/4 | Course label contrast measured at 7.23:1 on the canvas; named fields, pressed choices, live feedback and keyboard drawer focus verified. Screen-reader use is unverified. |\\r\\n| Performance | 3/4 | No runtime errors in the tested tab; bounded opacity/transform pulse. Final build passes, but broader application chunk warnings remain. No device performance benchmark was run. |\\r\\n| Responsive design | 3/4 | 320, 460, 768, 1280 and 1920px tested with no horizontal overflow; close button 44x44px and mobile course title 44px high. Physical devices and effective 200% text enlargement remain unverified. |\\r\\n| Theming | 3/4 | Scoped course tokens consistently style shell and portaled drawer; existing lesson palette retained. Light-only surface; dark integration is unverified. |\\r\\n| Anti-patterns | 4/4 | Progressive tasks, concrete authored prompts, one exercise surface and consistent sentence hierarchy. |\\r\\n| **Total** | **16/20** | **Good, up from 13/20** |\\r\\n\\r\\nIssues: **0 P0, 0 P1, 0 P2, 1 P3** in this scoped follow-up. Unverified checks are limits, not invented defects.\\r\\n\\r\\n## All six original findings are resolved\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Faint labels: shared muted token now renders rgb(66,84,75); measured contrast 7.229:1 on rgb(245,242,237).\\r\\n2. Saving ambiguity: landing and direct-run entry state that answers are not saved to the account and are cleared by reloading or leaving the course. Account chrome identifies assigned work on opening routes; persisted legacy lesson/practice statuses remain intact. Tests confirm mixed answers make zero account updates. Authenticated live backend behavior was not browser-tested.\\r\\n3. Inaccurate final action: pending fresh checks use the same selection as the progression logic. The final main question says Try a fresh case when required, or Review practice otherwise. Both branches are tested; browser traversed the fresh-case branch.\\r\\n4. Written-stage hierarchy: sentences render at 28px desktop and 23px mobile, including the guided diagram engine. A browser-discovered inherited inner wrapper background was removed. Fields and diagram slots keep their useful boundaries.\\r\\n5. Mobile controls: drawer Close is 44x44px; course-title link is 44px high. Escape closes the drawer and returns focus to Course menu.\\r\\n6. Mechanical copy: six sentence-specific prompts and targeted feedback replace generated indicator questions. Summary uses Who or what the rule covers. Final polish replaces complete-condition instructions with concrete directions about both sides of the arrow and the details that limit the rule.\\r\\n\\r\\n## One P3 refinement remains\\r\\n\\r\\n**[P3] Small secondary mobile labels**\\r\\n\\r\\n- Location: `opening-course.css:19`, `:30`, `:51`, `:87`.\\r\\n- Category: responsive typography.\\r\\n- Evidence: landing eyebrow is 12px at 320px; drawer categories and lesson navigation metadata are 10px in source. Main instructions, form labels and mobile progress text are at least 14px.\\r\\n- Impact: secondary categories are less comfortable for readers who need larger text. Contrast now passes; this is not a claim of a WCAG minimum-font-size violation.\\r\\n- Recommendation: evaluate 14px mobile metadata with tighter letter spacing against the complete course menu and navigation. Suggested command: `$impeccable typeset`, then `$impeccable polish`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification follows the requested order\\r\\n\\r\\nImplemented fixes, verified the complete flow, ran polish, then audited the final presentation. No design changes were made during this final audit.\\r\\n\\r\\n- Browser: Codex in-app browser only, temporary verification tab. Walked all 24 main questions, repaired a deliberately wrong qualification answer, completed its fresh check, inspected summary and retained review answers, and verified reload resets with an explicit notice. After final polish, freshly loaded the first three and all seven guided diagrams, then inspected the updated written stage and landing.\\r\\n- Responsive: final written stage scroll widths 305/445/753/1265/1905 at viewports 320/460/768/1280/1920. Mobile progress 14px, notice 14px, sentence 23px; larger sentence 28px.\\r\\n- Motion: Start practice pseudo-element uses oc-start-invitation normally and none under prefers-reduced-motion. Overrides were reset.\\r\\n- Tests: broad run 257 passes with one 15-second obligation-test timeout; focused rerun passed in 5.91s with 60-second allowance. Following final copy edits, all 18 affected tests across 3 files passed cleanly, including all 9 runner tests. Distinct covered tests: 258, not 276.\\r\\n- Full app TypeScript preflight passed before server startup. Final direct Vite build passed: 7,160 modules, 55.07s. Scoped lint:0 errors, 1 existing Fast Refresh warning.\\r\\n- Two fresh read-only reviews found no high-confidence logic defects. Review did not substitute for browser verification.\\r\\n- Screenshot: [polished written stage](C:/Users/Joshu/.codex/visualizations/2026/10/07/01a117ea-7c95-7813-af3b-866b05ec963a/conditional-polished-written-stage.png).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Release and evidence limits stay explicit\\r\\n\\r\\nLocal recovered checkout only. No commit, push, merge, publication, backend write or Lovable credit spend. Reviewed changes must be integrated with current main; never push the older recovered baseline. The launcher restarted the stopped local server through its typecheck gate. This browser session could not inspect the user\'s browser error page because the browser tool blocked its internal error-page protocol; the checked preview itself loaded and was tested in a separate tab.\\r\\n\\r\\nNo real phone/tablet, screen reader, effective 200% browser text enlargement, dark-mode integration, authenticated persistence, production performance or empirical retention study was completed. The OS text-scale command did not change computed web text size, so it is not counted as a successful text-enlargement test.\\r\\n\\r\\nChanges are hash-preserved in `2026-10-07-impeccable-fixes-overlay`: 11 delta files and 65-file inventory. Reloading or leaving the course clears them.\\";\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function OpeningCourseNavigation({ view, url }: { view: string; url: OpeningCourseUrl }) {\\r\\n  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);\\r\\n  const focusDestination = useRef(false);\\r\\n  function closeForNavigation() { focusDestination.current = open; setOpen(false); }\\r\\n  const navigation = <nav aria-label=\\"Conditional course\\">\\r\\n    <p>Intro path</p><Link aria-current={view === \'lessons\' || !!openingCourseStep(view) ? \'page\' : undefined} to={url(\'lessons\')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><BookOpen size={18}/>Lessons</Link>\\r\\n    <p>Practice</p><Link aria-current={view === \'practice\' ? \'page\' : undefined} to={url(\'practice\')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Sparkles size={18}/>Practice Drills</Link>\\r\\n    <Link aria-current={view === \'labs\' ? \'page\' : undefined} to={url(\'labs\')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Dumbbell size={18}/>Drill Lab</Link>\\r\\n    <p>Tools</p><Link aria-current={view === \'tools\' ? \'page\' : undefined} to={url(\'tools\')} onClick={closeForNavigation}><Bookmark size={18}/>Flashcards</Link>\\r\\n  </nav>;\\r\\n  return <>\\r\\n    <aside className=\\"oc-course-rail\\"><Link className=\\"oc-back\\" to=\\"/classroom\\"><ArrowLeft size={15}/>Classroom</Link><div className=\\"oc-brand\\">Conditional logic</div>{navigation}</aside>\\r\\n    <div className=\\"oc-mobile-nav\\"><Link to={url(\'lessons\')}>Conditional logic</Link><Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}><SheetTrigger asChild><button type=\\"button\\"><Menu size={18}/>Course menu</button></SheetTrigger><SheetContent side=\\"left\\" className=\\"oc-course-drawer\\" aria-describedby={undefined} onCloseAutoFocus={event => {\\r\\n      if (!focusDestination.current) return;\\r\\n      event.preventDefault();\\r\\n      focusDestination.current = false;\\r\\n      requestAnimationFrame(() => {\\r\\n        const heading = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(\'.oc-course-screen [data-course-heading] h1\');\\r\\n        if (heading) { heading.tabIndex = -1; heading.focus({ preventScroll: true }); }\\r\\n      });\\r\\n    }}><SheetTitle>Conditional logic</SheetTitle>{navigation}</SheetContent></Sheet></div>\\r\\n  </>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction CourseCard({ step, url }: { step: OpeningCourseStep; url: OpeningCourseUrl }) {\\r\\n  return <li className=\\"oc-path-card\\"><span className=\\"oc-label\\">{step.type}</span><h2>{step.title}</h2><p>{step.description}</p><Link className=\\"oc-action\\" to={url(step.id)}>{step.type === \'note\' ? <BookOpen size={16}/> : <Dumbbell size={16}/>} {step.type === \'note\' ? \'Read lesson\' : \'View drill\'}<ArrowRight size={16}/></Link></li>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nconst drillInstructions: Record<string, { summary: string; heading: string; steps: string[] }> = {\\r\\n  shuffleItems = true,\\r\\n  explanations = {},\\r\\n  embedded = false,\\r\\n  onResult,\\r\\n  feedbackForResult,\\r\\n}: DiagramDrillsProps = {}) {\\r\\n  const [queue, setQueue] = useState(() => shuffleItems ? shuffle(items) : [...items]);\\r\\n  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);\\r\\n  const [side, setSide] = useState<\\"left\\" | \\"right\\">(\\"left\\");\\r\\n  const [left, setLeft] = useState<string[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [right, setRight] = useState<string[]>([]);\\r\\n  const [checked, setChecked] = useState<null | boolean>(null);\\r\\n  const item = queue[index];\\r\\n  const done = index >= queue.length;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const reset = useCallback(() => { setLeft([]); setRight([]); setSide(\\"left\\"); setChecked(null); onResult?.(null); }, [onResult]);\\r\\n  const push = (token: string) => {\\r\\n    if (checked !== null) return;\\r\\n    (side === \\"left\\" ? setLeft : setRight)(v => [...v, token]);\\r\\n  };\\r\\n  const undo = () => (side === \\"left\\" ? setLeft : setRight)(v => v.slice(0, -1));\\r\\n\\r\\n  const check = () => {\\r\\n    const mine = { left: norm(left.join(\\" \\")), right: norm(right.join(\\" \\")) };\\r\\n    const target = { left: norm(item.left), right: norm(item.right) };\\r\\n    const contra = itemContrapositive(item);\\r\\n    const ok = (mine.left === target.left && mine.right === target.right)\\r\\n      || (allowContrapositive && mine.left === norm(contra.left) && mine.right === norm(contra.right));\\r\\n    setChecked(ok);\\r\\n    onResult?.({ itemId: item.id, left: mine.left, right: mine.right, correct: ok });\\r\\n    if (persistProgress) {\\r\\n      const saved = readSaved().diagram ?? { done: 0, correct: 0 };\\r\\n      writeSaved({ diagram: { done: saved.done + 1, correct: saved.correct + (ok ? 1 : 0) } });\\r\\n    }\\r\\n  };\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (done) {\\r\\n    return (\\r\\n      <section className=\\"cir-card\\">\\r\\n        <p className=\\"cir-prompt\\">All diagram drills done.</p>\\r\\n        <div className=\\"cir-actions\\">\\r\\n          <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-btn\\" onClick={() => { setQueue(shuffleItems ? shuffle(items) : [...items]); setIndex(0); reset(); }}>{shuffleItems ? \\"Shuffle again\\" : \\"Practice again\\"}</button>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </section>\\r\\n    );\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  const contra = itemContrapositive(item);\\r\\n  return (\\r\\n    <section className=\\"cir-card\\">\\r\\n      {!embedded && <><div className=\\"cir-bar\\"><span style={{ width: `${Math.round((index / queue.length) * 100)}%` }} /></div>\\r\\n      <p className=\\"cir-meta\\">Statement {index + 1} of {queue.length}. {allowContrapositive ? \\"Diagram it. Contrapositives count.\\" : \\"Build the original diagram.\\"}</p></>}\\r\\n      <p className=\\"cir-prompt\\">{item.prompt}</p>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cir-build\\">\\r\\n        <div className={`cir-slot${side === \\"left\\" ? \\" active\\" : \\"\\"}`} onClick={() => checked === null && setSide(\\"left\\")}>\\r\\n          {left.length ? left.map((t, i) => <Tok key={`${t}-${i}`} text={t} />) : <span className=\\"cir-stack-join\\">left</span>}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <span className=\\"cir-arrow\\">&rarr;</span>\\r\\n        <div className={`cir-slot${side === \\"right\\" ? \\" active\\" : \\"\\"}`} onClick={() => checked === null && setSide(\\"right\\")}>\\r\\n          {right.length ? right.map((t, i) => <Tok key={`${t}-${i}`} text={t} />) : <span className=\\"cir-stack-join\\">right</span>}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cir-side-toggle\\">\\r\\n        <button type=\\"button\\" aria-pressed={side === \\"left\\"} className={`cir-btn ${side === \\"left\\" ? \\"\\" : \\"ghost\\"}`} onClick={() => setSide(\\"left\\")}>{embedded ? \'Choose the left condition\' : \'Building left\'}</button>\\r\\n        <button type=\\"button\\" aria-pressed={side === \\"right\\"} className={`cir-btn ${side === \\"right\\" ? \\"\\" : \\"ghost\\"}`} onClick={() => setSide(\\"right\\")}>{embedded ? \'Choose the right condition\' : \'Building right\'}</button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cir-palette\\">\\r\\n        {item.names.flatMap(name => allowNegation ? [name, `/${name}`] : [name]).map(token => (\\r\\n          <button key={token} type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-token\\" aria-label={token.startsWith(\\"/\\") ? `Not ${token.slice(1)}` : token} disabled={checked !== null} onClick={() => push(token)}><Tok text={token} /></button>\\r\\n        ))}\\r\\n        {(item.connectors ?? []).map(c => (\\r\\n          <button key={c} type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-token\\" onClick={() => push(c)}>{c}</button>\\r\\n        ))}\\r\\n        <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-token\\" onClick={undo} disabled={checked !== null}>Undo</button>\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      <div className=\\"cir-actions\\">\\r\\n        {checked === null ? (\\r\\n          <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-btn\\" disabled={!left.length || !right.length} onClick={check}>{embedded ? \'Check answer\' : \'Check\'}</button>\\r\\n        ) : (\\r\\n          <>\\r\\n            <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-btn ghost\\" onClick={reset}>Try again</button>\\r\\n            {!embedded && <button type=\\"button\\" className=\\"cir-btn\\" onClick={() => { setIndex(i => i + 1); reset(); }}>Next</button>}\\r\\n          </>\\r\\n        )}\\r\\n      </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n      {checked !== null && (\\r\\n        <div className=\\"cir-feedback\\" role=\\"status\\">\\r\\n          {!embedded && <p>{checked ? \\"Right.\\" : \\"Not quite.\\"}</p>}\\r\\n          {feedbackForResult && <p>{feedbackForResult({ left: norm(left.join(\' \')), right: norm(right.join(\' \')), correct: checked })}</p>}\\r\\n          <p>Conditional: <Diagram left={item.left} right={item.right} /></p>\\r\\n          {explanations[item.id] && <p>{explanations[item.id]}</p>}\\r\\n          {allowContrapositive && <p>Contrapositive: <Diagram left={contra.left} right={contra.right} /></p>}\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n      )}\\r\\n    </section>\\r\\n  );\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}]
